information about this publication may be obtained from NISR:

-- 2 of 173 --

3
STATISTICAL YEARBOOK 2025
Foreword
Timely and updated statistics are vital for the planning, implementation, monitoring, and evaluation of development
programs at national, regional, and global levels, such as the National Strategy for Transformation (NST1), Vision 2050,
Sector Strategic Plans, and the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).
To meet the growing demand for high-quality data, the National Institute of Statistics of Rwanda (NISR) collaborates
closely with other members of the National Statistical System to produce reliable and timely statistics. These data are
derived from various sources, including administrative records, censuses, and surveys.
In this context, the Annual Statistical Yearbook (SYB) is published each year to provide a comprehensive compilation of
updated statistical information from multiple sectors and sources. The 2025 Statistical Yearbook covers a broad spectrum
of topics, including household living conditions, health, demography, labor force, gender, education, environment,
agriculture, water and energy, transportation, communication, travel and tourism, income, expenditure and wealth,
banking, insurance and finance, business enterprises and foreign trade, justice, and population, among others.
This Statistical Yearbook 2025 serves as a critical resource for policymakers, researchers, and stakeholders, offering
evidence-based information to support informed decision-making and policy development.
We extend our gratitude to both public and private institutions for their invaluable contributions to the production of
this report.
MURENZI Ivan
Director General
